# sw.sword — Brain function index (generated)
Every name registered into the DirectAI Lua brain by the C++ kernel (`brain.RegisterFunction` / `brain.RegisterMethod` in `src/libraries/simulation`).
Generated by `forge/tools/build_dec_index.py` from sw.sword **`master` @ `51bdc0ec9e`** (2026-11-05); usage counts from sw.models `master` @ `d2f78fafcd` and sw.soult `NavySmallUpdates` @ `c1b3ba6595`. Regenerate after pulling; line numbers drift.
**Stale check:** if `git -C C:/MASARepo/sw.sword rev-parse --short=10 HEAD` differs from the commit above, line numbers and counts are hints only: confirm with grep. **Grep this file, never read it whole.**
Columns: **Int** = occurrences in `data/app-data/resources/integration/*.lua`; **Models** = occurrences in sw.models `data/data/models/**/*.lua`; **Soult** = same in sw.soult; **txt** = listed in sw.models `decfunctions.txt` (used by `ant checkdec`). Names starting with `_DEC_` take `myself` (the agent's decision object) and are normally wrapped by a same-name-without-underscore Lua function in `DECFunctionWrappers.lua`; Lua code should call the wrapper.

Total registrations: 1636 — distinct names: 1619

Names registered more than once (13; same name bound in several places — last registration wins at runtime, check call order): `DEC_Agent_EstBrouille`, `DEC_Agent_EstEnSilenceRadioEmission`, `DEC_Agent_EstEnSilenceRadioReception`, `DEC_ConnaissanceObjet_Localisation`, `DEC_GetID`, `DEC_GetRawMission`, `DEC_Itineraire_DernierPoint`, `DEC_MissionParameter_CreateDirection`, `GetType`, `Getmunitions_`, `GetnbIT_`, `GetporteeAction_`, `_DEC_StartTirIndirectSurPositionAvecTypeCible`
